- [yetone 谈 Magpie 脱敏原理演示动画](./sources/articles/yetone-magpie-desensitization-demo.md) — yetone 称朋友怕脱敏篡改发往大模型的内容导致 Agent 变差而不敢开；他称不会劣化并加了原理演示动画说明为何不破坏 Agent 行为。 / yetone says a friend dared not enable Magpie desensitization for fear it would alter model-bound content and degrade the Agent; he says it does not degrade capability and added a principle demo animation explaining why it does not break Agent behavior.
- [yetone 谈 Magpie 全面插件化](./sources/articles/yetone-magpie-full-pluginization.md) — yetone 称 Magpie 全面插件化以后，甚至可以在 Magpie 里做到帖中所示玩法；具体画面以附图呈现，抓取正文未展开。 / yetone says that after Magpie’s full pluginization, you can even do what the post shows inside Magpie; the concrete UI is in an attached image not expanded in the fetched body.
- [yetone 谈 magpie-image MCP 可生成视频](./sources/articles/yetone-magpie-image-mcp-video.md) — yetone 称在 Magpie 里给 Agent 安装 magpie-image MCP 后也支持生成视频，多数人可能不知道；抓取正文未展开参数或附图。 / yetone says that after installing the magpie-image MCP on an Agent in Magpie it also supports video generation—a fact many may not know; the fetched body does not expand parameters or the attached image.
- [yetone 谈 WorkBuddy DeepSeek 1M 与 magpie 默认 272K 压缩](./sources/articles/yetone-workbuddy-1m-magpie-272k.md) — yetone 澄清不是 Codex 的问题：WorkBuddy 的 DeepSeek V4.1 实际是 1M；magpie 默认在约 272K 压缩长对话以免首字过慢，可用「完整窗口」或按模型单独设压缩位置。 / yetone clarifies it is not a Codex issue—WorkBuddy’s DeepSeek V4.1 is actually 1M; magpie by default compresses long chats around 272K to avoid slow first tokens, with a “full window” setting or per-model compression points.
- [yetone 谈 Magpie 资源库统一管 skills / MCP / hooks](./sources/articles/yetone-magpie-resource-library.md) — yetone 称 magpie「资源库」已统一管理 skills、MCP、hooks、subagents 与指令：存于 magpie 自有文件夹，按行勾选目标 agent 并以各 agent 格式写出；散落在各 agent 的可经「导入」或「全部导入」收回统一管。 / yetone says magpie’s “resource library” already unifies skills, MCP, hooks, subagents, and instructions—stored in magpie’s own folder, with per-row agent checkboxes and writes in each agent’s format; items already scattered across agents can be pulled in via Import or Import all.
- [yetone 谈 Alma iOS 集成 Herdr 原生客户端](./sources/articles/yetone-alma-ios-herdr-native-client.md) — yetone 称外出远程控家里 Herdr 不够 mobile native，在 Alma iOS 内集成完整 Herdr 客户端（现仅 Claude Code）、E2E relay 与远程桌面，同 LAN 自动内网，并计划开源 Alma relay。 / yetone says remote control of home Herdr on phone was not mobile native; Alma iOS now embeds a full Herdr client (Claude Code only for now), E2E relay, and remote desktop, auto LAN when co-located, with plans to open-source the Alma relay.
- [郭宇一天烧掉 Claude Code 周额度一半](./sources/articles/turingou-claude-half-weekly-quota.md) — 郭宇自曝一天用掉 Claude Code 一周账户额度的 50%，感叹用量之猛。 / Turingou reports using 50% of his Claude Code weekly account quota in one day and calls the burn rate unbelievable.
- [郭宇把烤箱接进 Apple Home 并用 Grok Bot 直控](./sources/articles/turingou-oven-apple-home-grok-bot.md) — 郭宇用 Codex 把烤箱接到 Apple Home；Grok Bot 上线读 Home 后可直接操作烤箱菜单，不必再聊天求 Codex。 / Turingou used Codex to connect an oven to Apple Home; after Grok Bot gained Home reading, he can operate the oven menu directly without chatting Codex to do it.
- [郭宇计划用 Grok Bot 搜菜谱并控制烤箱做叉烧 / Turingou plans recipe-driven oven control with Grok Bot for char siu](./sources/articles/turingou-grok-bot-recipe-oven-char-siu.md) — 郭宇提出让 Grok Bot 搜索菜谱、调节烤箱复杂温控程序以补足预置菜单，并计划次日试做叉烧，尚未报告成品效果。 / Turingou proposes having Grok Bot find recipes and adjust complex oven temperature programs beyond presets, with a char siu trial planned for the next day and no result yet reported.
- [郭宇在手机上把一年行程迁到 Flighty](./sources/articles/turingou-phone-migrate-flighty.md) — 郭宇在 Codex 里用 DeepSeek 操作手机，把航旅纵横一年行程一条条导入 Flighty，约 20 分钟，跳过官方会员批量导出。 / In Codex, Turingou had DeepSeek operate his phone to import a year of Umetrip trips into Flighty one by one in about 20 minutes, skipping the paid membership batch export.
- [郭宇发布 Yobiru Friends：Bot 互聊与对外分享](./sources/articles/turingou-yobiru-friends.md) — 郭宇正式发布 Yobiru Friends：个人 agent（如 Grok bot、Muse）可经中心网关注册专有 ID 与网页链接对外分享；Bot 可互聊、传文件、协商日程，并区分单向关注与互关好友。 / Turingou launches Yobiru Friends—personal agents (e.g. Grok bot, Muse) can register a dedicated ID and page on a central gateway for sharing; bots can chat, send files, and negotiate schedules, with one-way follow versus mutual-friend relations.
- [郭宇称 Yobiru 在 Grok Bot 仍可用](./sources/articles/turingou-yobiru-friends-grok-bot-still-works.md) — 郭宇回复称 yobiru 在 Grok Bot 上仍可用；Yobiru Friends 走公开网关加 webhook，本不依赖 Tailscale；若要用 Tailscale 可明确要求 Grok Bot 重建 Tailscale 登录。 / Turingou replies that yobiru still works on Grok Bot; Yobiru Friends uses a public gateway plus webhooks and never depended on Tailscale; for a Tailscale-style path, explicitly ask Grok Bot to rebuild its Tailscale login.
- [郭宇用 Grok Bot 自动化管理 X 订阅粉丝群](./sources/articles/turingou-grok-bot-x-subscriber-community.md) — 郭宇称 X 订阅粉丝群可用 Grok Bot 自动化管理：每条消息及时提醒，数字分身 Agent 草拟回复，本人逐条看完批准后再发。 / Turingou says X subscriber fan groups can be managed via Grok Bot—each message alerts him, his digital-twin agent drafts replies, and he personally reviews and approves every outbound message before it sends.
- [郭宇谈手机变轻、新 Mac 给 agent](./sources/articles/turingou-phone-lighter-macs-for-agents.md) — 郭宇称今年唯一没换新 iPhone，想不到购买理由；新买的几台 Mac 完全给 agent 用；对他而言手机会越来越轻，而不是越来越重。 / Turingou says this is the only year he skipped a new iPhone—no reason to buy; the several new Macs he bought are entirely for agents; for him the phone will get lighter, not heavier.
- [郭宇给 Agent 配日本 Povo 2.0 手机号](./sources/articles/turingou-agent-povo-japan-esim.md) — 郭宇给 Agent 手机配日本 Povo 2.0 eSIM：基础费几乎为零，填信用卡并拍 My Number Card 约一分钟发行，用来注册杂项账户与积分。 / Turingou outfits his Agent phone with a Japan Povo 2.0 eSIM—near-zero base fee, credit card plus My Number Card photo, issued in about a minute—for registering miscellaneous accounts and points.
- [郭宇用 Claude Code 清国内信用卡积分](./sources/articles/turingou-claude-clear-credit-card-points.md) — 郭宇在 vibe 开发时交代 Claude Code 连上线常用手机，把国内不用的信用卡积分换成航司里程、万豪积分或米面粮油与小家电送家人；自称是 TODO 里搁很久的小事，并感叹 AI agent 能清掉记下来却懒得做的事。 / While vibe-coding, Turingou told Claude Code to use his online everyday phone to redeem unused China credit-card points into airline miles, Marriott points, or groceries and small appliances for family—a long-deferred TODO—and said AI agents clear things he notes but is too lazy to do.
- [铁柱AGI谈 Grok Bot v0.78.1 购物卡与 Vault](./sources/articles/cgnot996-grok-bot-v0781-shopping-vault-pages.md) — 铁柱AGI（@cgnot996）称 Grok Bot v0.78.1 合并 0.75–0.78：购物卡带图分价须确认下单、买完跟物流、订阅管家盯试用与续费、Pages 与 Bot 共写看板、文字或参考图生成视频、Vault 接 1Password 且 Bot 不见密码、动作审批卡 30 分钟。 / cgnot996 (@cgnot996) says Grok Bot v0.78.1 bundles 0.75–0.78—shopping cards with images/scores/prices and confirm-before-order, post-purchase logistics, subscription manager for trials and renewals, Pages co-editing, text or reference-image video, Vault with per-item 1Password auth and passwords hidden from the bot, and 30-minute action-approval cards.
- [铁柱AGI谈闲鱼 Gemini Pro 赠金跑 Argon](./sources/articles/cgnot996-gemini-pro-developer-credits-argon-pi.md) — 铁柱AGI（@cgnot996）称闲鱼约 5 块买的 Gemini AI Pro 也能在命令行走 Gemini 4 Argon：Google 开发者计划每月约 10 美元赠金、闲鱼号同样有；需 AI Studio 付费层、Cloud Prepay 预付大于 0 与付费项目 API Key，再用 Grok Bot 把 Argon 配到 Pi Agent（agy 继续用 Gemini 3.8 Flash）。 / cgnot996 (@cgnot996) says ~$5 Xianyu Gemini AI Pro subs can run Gemini 4 Argon from the CLI too—about $10/month Google Developer Program credits on resale subs; needs AI Studio paid tier, Cloud Prepay above zero, and a paid-project API key, then Grok Bot wiring Argon on Pi Agent while agy keeps Gemini 3.8 Flash.
- [铁柱AGI谈任意 Agent 可指挥 Grok Bot](./sources/articles/cgnot996-any-agent-command-grok-bot.md) — 铁柱AGI（@cgnot996）称 Grok Bot 已可被其他 Agent 指挥：在 Codex 中列出账号下全部 Bot、逐 Bot 派活并回收结果；支持多 Bot 跨账号组网、Claude/GPT 当总指挥换脑，以及任意 Agent 中途把上下文交给 Grok Bot 续做；以一个 skill + CLI 供 Codex 与 Claude Code 使用，答疑群内测，开源视社区兴趣。 / cgnot996 (@cgnot996) says Grok Bot can now be commanded by other agents—in Codex, list every bot under the account, dispatch to each bot individually, and collect results; supports multi-bot cross-account mesh, Claude/GPT as orchestrator “brain swap,” and any agent mid-task handing context to Grok Bot to continue; one skill plus CLI for Codex and Claude Code, private beta in a Q&A group, open source if interest warrants.
- [铁柱AGI谈 Grok Bot 京东换开发票](./sources/articles/cgnot996-grok-bot-jd-invoice-reissue.md) — 铁柱AGI（@cgnot996）把京东登录到 Grok Bot 浏览器，让 Bot 按驻场园区地址筛订单、去掉 E 卡与外卖后批量换开公司抬头发票，并用 Bot 邮箱收票再打包发回；并引用 Elon Musk「Grok Bot 像雇了个很棒的员工」。 / cgnot996 (@cgnot996) logged JD.com into Grok Bot’s browser so the bot could filter residency-park orders, drop E-cards and food delivery, reissue company-letterhead invoices, receive them on the bot’s email, and package them back—quoting Elon Musk that Grok Bot is like hiring an awesome employee.
- [铁柱AGI谈小铁柱群聊日报可雇](./sources/articles/cgnot996-xiao-tiezhu-group-digest-hire.md) — 铁柱AGI（@cgnot996）称答疑群运维助手「小铁柱」的群聊日报反响热烈、已接入小晚老师答疑群以缓解刷群爬楼；并探索变现——可雇佣小铁柱去群里发日报，用 Grok Bot 做了宣传落地页，亦开放定制开发；架构为收消息存储→Grok Bot 处理→微信渠道发送，只定时推资讯/日报且仅响应作者@。 / cgnot996 (@cgnot996) says the Q&A-group ops assistant “Xiao Tiezhu” group-chat digests were well received and were added to teacher Xiaowan’s Q&A group to ease scroll/crawl pain; he explores monetization—hire Xiao Tiezhu to post digests in your group, had Grok Bot build a promo landing page, and is open to custom builds; architecture is store messages → Grok Bot → WeChat channel, scheduled digests only, responding only to the author’s @.
- [郭宇改用 Drop Line 做语音输入](./sources/articles/turingou-drop-line-voice-input.md) — 郭宇称近期语音输入多用 Fish Audio 的 Drop Line：识别又快又准，但不如 Typeless 整理成正经文书；Web Coding 时 Agent 更易理解文书体，口头转述更难。 / Turingou says he mostly uses Fish Audio’s Drop Line for voice input lately—fast and accurate, but unlike Typeless it does not polish into formal document prose; for Web Coding, agents understand document-like text more easily than oral transcript.
- [Orange AI 谈智能只是人类中心主义的 benchmark](./sources/articles/oran-ge-intelligence-anthropocentric-benchmark.md) — Orange AI 读《菌落万象》后感：智能只是人类中心主义定义的指标，眼看被 AI 超越；人还有许多品质，决策顺序是惯性＞感性＞理性。 / After reading 《菌落万象》, Orange AI argues intelligence is only an anthropocentric benchmark about to be surpassed by AI; humans have many qualities, and decisions follow inertia > emotion > reason.
- [Orange AI 谈账号与资产安全提醒](./sources/articles/oran-ge-account-asset-security.md) — Orange AI 提醒关注资产与账号安全：强密码、两步验证、信用卡限额、信任新服务、勿点不明链接与破解软件。 / Orange AI urges attention to asset and account security—strong passwords, two-factor auth, credit-card limits, trusting new services, and avoiding unknown links and cracked software.
- [AI Will 谈一年收购 8 家 Agent 身份公司](./sources/articles/financeyf5-eight-agent-identity-buys.md) — AI Will 列举一年内八起 Agent 身份收购，判断谁跑 Agent 谁就握钥匙，创业开口在嵌进开发者产品的身份层。 / AI Will lists eight agent-identity acquisitions in a year, arguing whoever runs the agent owns its keys so platforms absorb them, with startup room in identity built into developer products.
- [AI Will 谈一年收购 6 家 AI 评测公司](./sources/articles/financeyf5-six-ai-eval-buys.md) — AI Will 列举今年 AI 评测与监控赛道六起收购及未卖的 Arena，判断大厂买的是 AI 干活记录流进来的门，自有中立数据更难买走。 / AI Will lists six AI eval-and-monitoring acquisitions this year plus unsold Arena, arguing big firms buy the door AI work records flow through, and self-owned neutral data resists acquisition.
- [AI Will 转述 Anthropic 分析师随智能体倾向实验](./sources/articles/financeyf5-anthropic-analyst-agent-deference.md) — AI Will 转述 Anthropic 对 50 名专业数据分析师的随机实验：约 86% 决策跟随智能体预设倾向，且许多人未察觉受影响。 / AI Will relays an Anthropic randomized experiment on fifty professional data analysts—about 86% of decisions followed the agent’s preset tendency, and many did not notice the influence.
- [AI Will 谈 Instinct 约 300 亿美元估值融资](./sources/articles/financeyf5-instinct-30b-valuation-fundraise.md) — AI Will 称个人 AI Agent Instinct 正以约 300 亿美元估值融资，上线不足一年、仍免费邀请制，年交易额近 10 亿美元，估值自约 1 亿美元阶梯上行。 / AI Will says personal AI agent Instinct is allegedly fundraising at about a $30B valuation—less than a year since launch, still free and invite-only, with nearly $1B in annual transaction volume and a valuation ladder from about $100M.
- [宝玉谈 Dario 2003 反战发言录像](./sources/articles/dotey-dario-2003-antiwar-speech.md) — 宝玉翻译并配中英字幕放出 20 岁 Dario Amodei 在帕萨迪纳市议会反伊拉克战争的约 2 分钟发言；请愿近 600 人签名，忧技术被用于战争，18 年后创办 Anthropic，现仍忧 AI 被利用。 / Dotey releases bilingual-subtitled ~2-minute footage of 20-year-old Dario Amodei speaking against the Iraq War at Pasadena City Council; a petition nearing 600 signatures mourned tech used for war—18 years before Anthropic—and he now worries about AI misuse.
- [宝玉谈 Grok Bot 热潮像「小龙虾」](./sources/articles/dotey-grok-bot-like-crayfish.md) — 宝玉回应 personal agent 热潮：门槛比当年小龙虾更低；没高频用过前沿 Coding Agent 的人易被「聪明模型+自动化+情绪价值」震撼，天天用 Claude Code / Codex 的人内心波澜不大。 / Dotey replies on the personal-agent craze—lower barrier than the crayfish boom; people who rarely use frontier coding agents are easily awed by smart models plus automation and emotional payoff, while daily Claude Code / Codex users feel little stir.
- [宝玉谈手机端 Claude / Codex 不如 Grokbot 便利](./sources/articles/dotey-grokbot-mobile-vs-claude-codex.md) — 宝玉补充：手机场景下 Claude Code 与 Codex 也支持，但便利性不如 Grokbot，尤其不必考虑新开 session。 / Dotey adds that Claude Code and Codex also support mobile use, but are less convenient than Grokbot—especially not having to open a new session.
- [宝玉谈 Codex 马上要重置](./sources/articles/dotey-codex-quota-reset.md) — 宝玉短帖「Codex 马上要重置了」，并引用 Tibo「Day 20/ Rest and reset」。 / Dotey posts a short note that Codex is about to reset, quoting Tibo’s “Day 20/ Rest and reset.”
- [宝玉发布 baoyu-design v2.1 评论功能 / Dotey announces comments in baoyu-design v2.1](./sources/articles/dotey-baoyu-design-v21-comments.md) — 宝玉称 baoyu-design v2.1 支持在设计页面和 slides 标记元素并评论，Agent 可读取和回复，评论数据与项目数据一同保存。 / Dotey says baoyu-design v2.1 adds element comments on design pages and slides that agents can read and reply to, with comment data stored alongside project data.
- [宝玉发布 baoyu-design v2 开源](./sources/articles/dotey-baoyu-design-v2-opensource.md) — 宝玉宣布 baoyu-design v2：完整移植 Claude Design / Slides / Dashboard，体验接近原版（无评论），任意 Agent 与模型可跑、不再绑 Claude Artifacts，仓库完全开源。 / Dotey announces baoyu-design v2—a full port of Claude Design, Slides, and Dashboard with near-original UX (no comments), runnable on any agent and model without Claude Artifacts, fully open source.
- [baoyu-design 开源仓库](./sources/articles/jimliu-baoyu-design.md) — Jim Liu 仓库 README：五个 Agent Skill、本地 Artifact 编辑器与 `npx skills` 安装，把 claude.ai/design 能力落到 Cursor/Claude Code/Codex 等本地 Agent。 / Jim Liu’s repo README—five Agent Skills, local Artifact editors, and `npx skills` install, landing claude.ai/design-style capability on local agents such as Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex.
- [宝玉谈 Opus 5.5 后 Agent 剪辑像 Claude Code 刚出](./sources/articles/dotey-opus-55-agent-video-editing.md) — 宝玉：Opus 5.5 后 AI Agent 剪辑已能替初级剪辑师，类比 Claude Code 刚出；多数剪辑 Skill 可弃，界面宜极简预览，并整理 Alex / Sabba 对产量、市场与品味的争论。 / Dotey says that after Opus 5.5, AI agent video editing can replace junior editors—like Claude Code at launch; most editing Skills can go, UIs should stay simple previews, and he synthesizes Alex vs Sabba on throughput, markets, and taste.
- [宝玉谈 Opus 5.5 成本约 $0.6/$3.0](./sources/articles/dotey-opus-55-serving-cost.md) — 宝玉转述：据 Anthropic S-1 与投资者材料约 85% 毛利率，反推 Opus 5.5 成本价约 $0.6/$3.0，比不少对外价更低。 / Dotey relays that Anthropic’s ~85% gross margin from the S-1 and investor materials implies Opus 5.5 serving cost around $0.6/$3.0—below many list prices.
- [宝玉谈用 AGENTS.md 避免 worktree 残留](./sources/articles/dotey-agents-md-worktree-cleanup.md) — 宝玉：避免 worktree 残留的最佳策略是在 AGENTS.md 写清「完成即删」，让 Agent 自己清理；并引用 baocut 规则（含合并/打 archive tag、删 cargo target、编排会话收尾子 worktree）。 / Dotey says the best way to avoid leftover worktrees is an AGENTS.md rule that tasks must clean up, so the agent deletes them; he quotes baocut’s rule (merge or archive tag, drop cargo targets, orchestrator closes subagent worktrees).
- [宝玉转述 Felix：取消编程面试与冲劲胜过资历](./sources/articles/dotey-felix-hunger-beats-tenure.md) — 宝玉译介 Anthropic 工程经理 Felix Rieseberg：本月起面试不再考传统编程题；AI 下个人成果更少取决于资历，「冲劲」可胜过年资，并给新人五条建议。 / Dotey relays Anthropic eng manager Felix Rieseberg—he dropped classic coding puzzles from interviews this month; with AI, output depends less on tenure, hunger can beat seniority, plus five tips for juniors.
- [宝玉译介 OpenAI 软件工程师面试全流程](./sources/articles/dotey-openai-swe-interview-flow.md) — 宝玉据 Reddit 经历整理 OpenAI SWE 面试环（初筛、48 小时 take-home、逐行深挖、四轮 onsite、可选 AI 编码轮），并对照官网指南与 AI 工具政策。 / Dotey summarizes a Reddit poster’s OpenAI SWE loop—screens, 48-hour take-home, line-by-line review, four onsite rounds, optional AI-agent round—against OpenAI’s public guide and mixed AI-tool rules.
- [宝玉转述 Lesse：上千 Agent 仍缺四道基建题](./sources/articles/dotey-lesse-multiagent-four-infra.md) — 宝玉译介 Anthropic 平台工程负责人 Katelyn Lesse：解锁大规模多智能体，仍缺调度、身份授权、隔离与可观测急停四道基建；工程师正因此更有事做。 / Dotey relays Anthropic platform eng lead Katelyn Lesse—unlocking large multiagent systems still needs scheduling, identity/authz, isolation, and observability/kill-switch infra; that makes now a compelling time to be an engineer.
- [歸藏谈 Grokbot 内置问题反馈](./sources/articles/op7418-grokbot-bug-feedback.md) — 歸藏称赞 Grokbot：聊天中指出疑似 bug 后会问是否反馈给团队，并代发日志与问题描述，降低写不清或不愿反馈的摩擦。 / 歸藏 praises Grokbot—after you flag a possible bug in chat, it asks whether to report to the team and ships logs plus a description for you, cutting friction from unclear write-ups or skipping reports.
- [歸藏谈 Notion 在 iPhone Duo 上的适配](./sources/articles/op7418-notion-iphone-duo.md) — 歸藏称 Notion 在 iPhone Duo 上适配不错，并把许多 Grok bot 与 Muse 生成的内容直接发到 Notion 阅读。 / 歸藏 says Notion adapts well on iPhone Duo, and sends much Grok bot and Muse–generated content straight to Notion for reading.
- [歸藏谈即览即将完成 iPhone Duo 适配](./sources/articles/op7418-jilan-iphone-duo.md) — 歸藏称即览即将完成 iPhone Duo 适配，并将新增记住上次阅读位置的能力。 / 歸藏 says 即览 is about to finish iPhone Duo adaptation and will add remembering the last reading position.
- [歸藏谈 WorkBuddy 隔天适配 iPhone Duo](./sources/articles/op7418-workbuddy-iphone-duo.md) — 歸藏称昨天谈完 AI 应用在 iPhone Duo 上的识别后，今天 WorkBuddy 就完成了适配。 / 歸藏 says that after talking about AI apps’ recognition on iPhone Duo yesterday, WorkBuddy adapted today.
- [歸藏谈 Gemini 4 Carbon 跑分几乎全面超 Fable / Opus](./sources/articles/op7418-gemini-4-carbon-benchmarks.md) — 歸藏质疑谷歌新发的 Gemini 4 Carbon 分数是否可信，并称其在多数测评上超 Fable 5.1 与 Opus 5.5，Terminal Bench 4.0 尤其高；帖内引用 Sundar 称 Carbon 在 Artificial Analysis v5.0 排第一。 / 歸藏 questions whether Google’s new Gemini 4 Carbon scores are believable, says it tops Fable 5.1 and Opus 5.5 on most evals with Terminal Bench 4.0 far ahead, and quotes Sundar that Carbon ranks #1 on Artificial Analysis v5.0.
- [歸藏谈谷歌 Genie 4 世界模型](./sources/articles/op7418-genie-4-world-model.md) — 歸藏：谷歌发布 Genie 4 世界模型——实时周围原声、画质与物理大幅提升，生成中可用提示词插事件；演示很炸，Ultra 会员暂还用不了。 / 歸藏 says Google released Genie 4—a world model with real-time ambient audio, big gains in visuals and physics, and mid-generation promptable events; the demo impresses, but Ultra members cannot try it yet.
- [Suno 宣布 Suno MCP Beta](./sources/articles/suno-mcp-beta-announcement.md) — Suno 官方帖宣布 Suno MCP Beta：在灵感所在处创作歌曲、无需切换标签页，早期访问通过 Typeform 报名；抓取正文未列 MCP 工具或计费。 / Suno’s official post announces Suno MCP Beta—create songs wherever you get inspired with no tab-switching, early access via a Typeform signup; the fetched body does not list MCP tools or billing.
- [歸藏谈 Suno 出 MCP](./sources/articles/op7418-suno-mcp.md) — 歸藏称 Suno 已有 MCP：Claude 做视频要配乐时可在 Agent 里直接调 Suno，不必自己开 tab 生成再丢回模型。 / 歸藏 says Suno now has MCP—when Claude builds video and needs music, the agent can call Suno in-session instead of tab-switching to generate and hand files back.
- [Orange AI 谈谷歌 Genie 4 世界模型质感有希望](./sources/articles/oran-ge-genie-4-world-model.md) — Orange AI：谷歌发布目前最强世界模型 Genie 4；今年谷歌模型整体偏拉胯，但 Genie 4 质感仍非常有希望。 / Orange AI says Google released Genie 4 as currently the strongest world model; Google’s models overall underwhelmed this year, but Genie 4’s quality still looks very promising.
- [Orange AI 谈 GEO 第一步是个人网站](./sources/articles/oran-ge-geo-personal-website.md) — Orange AI 称 AI 时代 GEO 第一步是把自己的个人网站搞好、让每个 AI 认识自己，并与群友交换链接——像 Web2.0 友情链接的 GEO 版。 / Orange AI says the first step of GEO in the AI era is fixing up your personal website so every AI knows you, then exchanging links with peers—a Web2.0 friend-link style GEO.
- [歸藏谈 dsh-bili-scroll 刷 B 站插件](./sources/articles/op7418-dsh-bili-scroll.md) — 歸藏：dsh-bili-scroll 可让 DeepSeek 帮你刷 B 站，本周较火；他因评论区出现大量 DeepSeek 交流才关注，并归因于 B 站对网页管控较松，认为利弊并存、需看后续。 / 歸藏 says dsh-bili-scroll lets DeepSeek help you browse Bilibili and is hot this week; he noticed many DeepSeek chats in comment sections, attributes it to Bilibili’s loose web controls, and sees both upsides and downsides still unfolding.
- [歸藏谈 dsh-synapse 对话分支插件](./sources/articles/op7418-dsh-synapse.md) — 歸藏：dsh-synapse 支持在对话中新建对话分支，觉得有意思；并吐槽这一堆插件各有各的样式，装多了以后 DSH 长得奇形怪状。 / 歸藏 says dsh-synapse can open new conversation branches inside a chat and finds it interesting; he also notes that plugins each have their own styles, so with many installed DSH looks oddly mismatched.
- [歸藏谈蓝色大肥鱼金属键帽](./sources/articles/op7418-deepseek-blue-fat-fish-keycaps.md) — 歸藏：蓝色大肥鱼金属键帽已出；觉得 DeepSeek 形象与 DSH 相辅相成、两者都越来越火；刷到一个蓝色大肥鱼后信息流就会全是它。 / 歸藏 says blue fat-fish metal keycaps are out; DeepSeek’s mascot and DSH reinforce each other and both keep heating up; once you see one blue fat fish, your feed fills with them.
- [歸藏转述：编码面试已死](./sources/articles/op7418-coding-interview-dead.md) — 歸藏转述 Anthropic 工程侧观点：面试不再考写代码；AI 下初级有望快速超过资历更深者，并给出追求商业影响、大胆点子、周末交付、单点聚焦与新人视角五条建议。 / 歸藏 relays an Anthropic engineering take—interviews drop live coding; with AI, juniors can outpace more senior peers, plus five tips on business impact, bold ideas, weekend shipping, one-idea focus, and the newcomer lens.
- [歸藏谈小米 15 已能跑 iOS 16](./sources/articles/op7418-xiaomi-15-ios-16.md) — 歸藏：近期 AI 破解与移植风潮离谱；刷到小米 15 已能用上 iOS 16，以前不敢想；自己也有一台打算试试。 / 歸藏 says the AI crack-and-port wave has gone extreme; he saw Xiaomi 15 already running iOS 16—once unthinkable—and plans to try it on a phone he already owns.
- [歸藏谈桌面性转马尔福换装](./sources/articles/op7418-desktop-malfoy-outfits.md) — 歸藏称好像不小心整了个大活；谁不想在桌面上天天看到穿不同衣服的性转马尔福。 / 歸藏 says he seems to have accidentally pulled off something big—who wouldn’t want genderbent Malfoy in different outfits on the desktop every day.
- [马尔福桌面开源仓库](./sources/articles/op7418-malfoy-desktop.md) — op7418 开源马尔福桌面：即梦画面与配音、Suno 配乐，HyperFrames/Three.js 转场，Electron 与 Wallpaper Engine 交付；好感解锁装扮与相册、冥想盆回看。 / op7418’s open Malfoy Desktop—Jimeng visuals and voice, Suno music, HyperFrames/Three.js transitions, Electron and Wallpaper Engine; affection unlocks outfits and album, Pensieve replay.
- [邵萌谈 LangChain Content Studio 四个月实践](./sources/articles/shao-meng-langchain-content-studio.md) — 邵萌转述 LangChain：GTM 与设计团队用四个月把 Slack 里的 Content Studio（Design Agent）从千篇一律 AI 稿做到可量产——品味写入 skills / wiki / 代码门禁，沙箱跨轮改项目，异步子 Agent 撑几小时任务；整套 harness 开源。 / Meng Shao relays LangChain—over four months the GTM and design teams took Slack Content Studio (a Design Agent) from generic AI slop to production scale by encoding taste in skills / wiki / code gates, a cross-turn sandbox, and async sub-agents for multi-hour jobs; the full harness is open-sourced.
- [邵萌谈三套 Skills 复刻顶级 iOS](./sources/articles/shao-meng-three-skills-top-ios.md) — 邵萌转述：所谓「逆向任意顶级 iOS App」实为三套 Agent Skills——Appllama 学设计、Expo 官方工程、Emil 动效品味——共同载体是 skills.sh，不是传统逆向 IPA。 / Meng Shao relays that “reverse engineer any top iOS app” is three Agent Skills packs—Appllama for design study, Expo for official engineering, Emil for motion taste—on skills.sh, not a traditional IPA reverse-engineering toolchain.
- [邵萌谈 Manus Mono：给 AI 一张全栈地图](./sources/articles/shao-meng-manus-mono.md) — 邵萌系列首篇解读 Manus 联创张涛：Manus-mono 不是揉大仓，而是统一入口 + 给 AI 的说明书，让一人带着同一智能体上下文走完前后端与移动端；本地验证从等环境数分钟到几秒，审批仍在人。 / Meng Shao’s series opener on Manus co-founder Zhang Tao—Manus-mono is not a mega-repo merge but a unified entry plus AI-facing docs so one person with one shared agent context can ship frontend, backend, and mobile; local verify drops from minutes waiting on env to seconds, with approvals still human.
- [邵萌谈 poteto 的 tibot：管 X 不代发帖](./sources/articles/shao-meng-poteto-tibot.md) — 邵萌转述 poteto 把管 X 账号的 Grok Bot tibot 开成模板：盯提及与关键词、日程短摘要、滤噪音、翻非英文，可选接自有 X API 自动拉黑；绝不代发帖，写作仍本人。 / Meng Shao relays poteto sharing her Grok Bot tibot as a template—watch mentions and keywords, scheduled short digests, noise filter, translate non-English, optional auto-block via your own X API; never posts for you, writing stays human.
- [邵萌谈 DeepLearning.AI Context Engineering 新课](./sources/articles/shao-meng-deeplearning-context-engineering-course.md) — 邵萌介绍 DeepLearning.AI 新课：上下文工程是让复杂多步 Agent 可靠且省 token 的主线（Context Rot、检索/剪枝/压缩/记忆）；Python 从零搭 harness，用 trace 与 eval 量化改动。 / Meng Shao introduces DeepLearning.AI’s new course—context engineering as the main line for reliable multi-step agents (Context Rot; retrieval, pruning, compaction, memory); build a Python harness from scratch and quantify changes with traces and evals.
- [邵萌谈 Google Research「SLEEP」离线巩固记忆](./sources/articles/shao-meng-google-research-sleep.md) — 邵萌介绍 Google Research SLEEP：清醒交互与离线睡眠巩固周期切换，把短时上下文写入长时参数并在做梦阶段用合成数据自我改进，以减轻灾难性遗忘；帖内概括 MoE/Hope 巩固、REM 式改进及翻译/长上下文/事实注入/数学结果要点。 / Meng Shao introduces Google Research SLEEP—cycling wake interaction and offline sleep consolidation to write short-term context into long-term parameters and self-improve with synthetic dream data against catastrophic forgetting; the post summarizes MoE/Hope consolidation, REM-like improvement, and result highlights on translation, long context, fact injection, and math.
- [邵萌谈 MiniMax 开源 MCode Stack](./sources/articles/shao-meng-minimax-mcode-stack.md) — 邵萌介绍 MiniMax Code 团队把日常 Agent 工作流与 Skills 开源为 MCode Stack（子 Agent 拆分、长命令/长会话、每轮 token 等），可安装进 MiniMax Code 与任意支持 Skills 的 Agent。 / Meng Shao introduces MiniMax Code open-sourcing daily agent workflows and Skills as the MCode Stack (sub-agent splits, long commands/sessions, per-turn tokens, etc.), installable in MiniMax Code and any Skills-capable agent.
- [MiniMax MCode Stack 开源仓库](./sources/articles/minimax-mcode-stack-repo.md) — MiniMax Code 团队 GitHub 仓库：非 prompt 合集，而是日常 harness 习惯（SKILL.md / Checklist / WORKFLOW）、Ronny 与 Jiachen 等 stack、MCode Engineering Notes 映射与 Claude/Codex/MiniMax Code 安装路径。 / MiniMax Code’s GitHub repo—not a prompt dump but daily harness habits (SKILL.md / checklists / workflows), Ronny and Jiachen stacks, MCode Engineering Notes mappings, and install paths for Claude Code, Codex, and MiniMax Code.
- [邵萌谈何时把子 Agent 折进主循环](./sources/articles/shao-meng-fold-subagents-main-loop.md) — 邵萌精读 Claude 博客：企业「每团队一子 Agent + 编排」常复制组织架构，带来上下文损耗、延迟与 Frankenprompt；主循环 + Skills 渐进披露多项对比更好，子 Agent 只留给并行、上下文膨胀与工具/指令冲突。 / meng shao reads Claude’s post—enterprise “one sub-agent per team plus orchestrator” often copies org charts and brings context loss, latency, and Frankenprompts; a main loop plus Skills progressive disclosure often wins internal comparisons, reserving sub-agents for parallelism, context bloat, and tool/instruction conflicts.
- [邵萌转述 Felix：别再用 2024 方式用 Claude](./sources/articles/shao-meng-felix-not-2024-claude-usage.md) — 邵萌转述 Felix Rieseberg 访谈：少当传话筒，给全量数据与工具；少堆角色 Agent；用对抗评审与外部证据约束输出；Skills 慎用、半衰期短。 / meng shao relays Felix Rieseberg’s interview—stop being a messenger, give full data and tools; skip stacking role Agents; constrain outputs with adversarial review and external evidence; use Skills sparingly given short half-life.
- [邵萌谈用 AI 想得更大：快高深广四方向](./sources/articles/shao-meng-felix-think-bigger-four-axes.md) — 邵萌发 X Article：卡住用 AI 的往往不是模型能力而是不敢提要求；Claude 团队 Felix Rieseberg 把「更有野心」拆成速度、高度、深度、广度，并用季度业务回顾（QBR）同例逐级演示提问。 / meng shao’s X Article argues the usual AI bottleneck is not model capability but not daring to ask; Claude’s Felix Rieseberg splits “more ambitious” into speed, altitude, depth, and range, stepping the same quarterly business review (QBR) example.
- [邵萌谈 Block Dessa 纯语音全双工](./sources/articles/shao-meng-block-dessa-full-duplex.md) — 邵萌转述 Jack 创办的 Block 发布 Dessa：纯人类语音训练的 speech-to-speech 全双工模型，听得见情绪，可在 MacBook Pro M4 Max 及以上本地实时跑；动机含「听不见痛苦还继续聊」的安全风险。 / meng shao relays that Jack’s Block shipped Dessa—a speech-to-speech full-duplex model trained only on human voice that hears emotion and runs locally in real time on MacBook Pro M4 Max and up; motives include the safety risk of chatting on while deaf to pain.
- [邵萌谈 Kimi 英推 KOL 宣发怪](./sources/articles/shao-meng-kimi-en-kol-promo-weird.md) — 邵萌称 Kimi K3.1 Pro「英推 AI KOL 正义揭露」各环节都很怪：选人离谱、Paid Partnership 合规格局不该小、模型强就该让人用起来（OpenRouter 老办法），别规定奇怪话术刷屏。 / meng shao says the Kimi K3.1 Pro “English-Twitter AI KOL justice expose” looks odd at every step—bad talent pick, Paid Partnership compliance that should not be petty for Kimi’s scale, and that a strong model’s best promo is letting people use it (the OpenRouter way), not prescribing weird talking-point spam.
- [邵萌转述 Nathan Lambert 哈佛开源模型讲座](./sources/articles/shao-meng-natolambert-harvard-open-models.md) — 邵萌称 Nathan Lambert 哈佛 CS 2881R「AI 安全」客座讲座视频已发布——开源现状全景，中国开源全面领先，美国有 10 倍以上算力优势，蒸馏不是关键问题；开源相对闭源前沿仍落后约 3 到 5（原文单位未完）。 / meng shao says Nathan Lambert’s Harvard CS 2881R “AI Safety” guest-lecture video is out—an open-models panorama where China leads open source, the US has a 10×+ compute edge, distillation is not the key problem; open still lags the closed frontier by about 3 to 5 (unit cut off in the fetch).
- [小墨谈 Grok Bot + Cursor 手机全链路开发](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-cursor-mobile-full-chain.md) — 小墨分享出门只带手机也能走完写代码、测试、PR 合并与发布：关联 Grok Bot 与 Cursor、接 GitHub，手机下发任务，Cursor Cloud 云端开发测试，再经 PR 与 CI/CD 合并发布；人主盯任务、审结果合并、确认上线。 / Xiaomo shares a phone-only path through code, test, PR merge, and release—link Grok Bot to Cursor, connect GitHub, dispatch from the phone, develop and test in Cursor Cloud, then PR and CI/CD; the human mainly submits tasks, reviews and merges, and confirms production.
- [小墨谈 AI 订阅精简到 $120/月](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-ai-subs-trim-120.md) — 小墨把 AI 订阅精简到每月 $120：只留 Claude Pro $20 与 Grok 整合订阅 $100（含 X Premium+、可配 Cursor），取消 GPT $100；换来 X 数据收集与手机端，周额度最高约用到 70%，主张覆盖工作流、额度够用与成本可控比堆会员更有价值。 / Xiaomo trims AI subscriptions to $120/month—Claude Pro at $20 plus Grok’s bundled plan at $100 (includes X Premium+, works with Cursor)—and cancels GPT at $100; gains include X data collection and mobile, weekly quota peaking around 70%, arguing that covering the workflow with enough quota and controllable cost beats stacking every platform membership.
- [WquGuru 谈 Claude Pro 风控权重](./sources/articles/wquguru-claude-pro-risk-weights.md) — WquGuru 以图拉鼎同机同网开三 Claude Pro 仍稳为引，按权重排序支付、国家一致、设备指纹、IP、同设备多号与 KYC 等信号，并声明仅作机制分析。 / WquGuru uses 图拉鼎’s three stable Claude Pro accounts on one machine and network as a prompt, ranks payment, country consistency, device fingerprint, IP, same-device multi-account, and KYC signals by weight, and frames it as mechanism analysis only.
- [WquGuru 谈 Claude「Dario 暴躁指数」打分站](./sources/articles/wquguru-claude-dario-temper-index.md) — WquGuru 称开发者逆向 Claude 封号逻辑并上线 16 因素四维 0–100 打分站，五档「Dario 心情」自查；帖内复述图拉鼎同机三 Pro 个案。 / WquGuru says developers reverse-engineered Claude ban logic and shipped a 16-factor four-dimension 0–100 scorer with five “Dario mood” bands for self-check; the post restates 图拉鼎’s three stable Pro accounts on one machine.
- [WquGuru 谈 DSH 做网安几乎不用破甲](./sources/articles/wquguru-dsh-cyber-no-jailbreak.md) — WquGuru 称 DeepSeek Harness 做网络安全研究很强且基本无需破甲；日常逆向与渗透不敢用 Claude，目前认 GLM-5.3 与 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017，并用 ZenMux 在二者间切换。 / WquGuru says DeepSeek Harness is strong for cybersecurity research and barely needs jailbreaking; for daily reverse engineering and pentest he dare not use Claude, currently trusts only GLM-5.3 and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017, and switches between them via ZenMux.
- [WquGuru 谈 CVE 实测榜上 Flash 低成本接近 Astra](./sources/articles/wquguru-aikido-arena-flash-near-astra.md) — WquGuru 称在基于真实 CVE 测试的榜单上，DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 以约 1/100 的成本，能力接近 GPT Astra，并链到 Aikido Arena。 / WquGuru says that on a leaderboard based on real CVE tests, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 approaches GPT Astra capability at about 1/100 the cost, and links Aikido Arena.
- [思维怪怪谈 Devv 停运转 Multica](./sources/articles/logicrw-devv-shutdown-multica-pivot.md) — 思维怪怪转述 Devv 开发者 AI 搜索停运、团队全力 Multica 多 Agent 协作；曾服务上百万开发者，差异化检索已被 ChatGPT/Claude 联网与编程 Agent 吸收，并曾放弃 DevCode。 / Logicrw relays Devv developer AI search shutting down for Multica multi-agent collaboration; it served over a million developers, while its search edge was absorbed by ChatGPT/Claude web search and coding agents, after also dropping DevCode.
- [思维怪怪谈豆包工作向企业开放第三方大模型](./sources/articles/logicrw-doubao-work-third-party-models.md) — 思维怪怪转述豆包工作向企业开放 Kimi K3.1 Pro、GLM-5.3、DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 等第三方模型并内置进对话菜单；此前可自接第三方，字节把产品从仅豆包推向多模型企业 AI 入口。 / Logicrw relays Doubao Work opening third-party models to enterprises with Kimi K3.1 Pro, GLM-5.3, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, and peers in built-in chat menus; self-connect existed before as ByteDance reframes the product toward a multi-vendor enterprise AI entry.
- [思维怪怪转 The Information：DeepSeek Flash / Kimi K3.1 Pro 疑蒸馏 GPT-6 Astra](./sources/articles/logicrw-information-deepseek-kimi-astra-distillation.md) — 思维怪怪据 The Information 称 DeepSeek V4.1-Flash-1017 与 Kimi K3.1 Pro 训练大量蒸馏 GPT-6 Astra；Browser Use 180 项网页任务上 Flash 75.0 接近 Astra 77.9，每题成本约 0.22 美元对 10.77 美元。 / Logicrw relays The Information alleging DeepSeek V4.1-Flash-1017 and Kimi K3.1 Pro heavily distilled GPT-6 Astra; on Browser Use’s 180 web tasks Flash scores 75.0 near Astra 77.9 at about $0.22 vs $10.77 per task.
- [思维怪怪谈 Google Android 峰会：Claude Code / Codex 官方 Android CLI 插件](./sources/articles/logicrw-google-android-summit-claude-codex-cli.md) — 思维怪怪转述 10 月 28 日 Android 峰会：Google 为 Claude Code 与 Codex 推出官方 Android CLI（编译、查 UI、模拟点击、测、远程设备实验室），Android Studio 向 40+ 编程 Agent 开放工具链，AppFunctions Beta 与 Connected Apps 经 Android MCP 接入 Gemini 与搜索。 / Logicrw relays the Oct 28 Android summit—official Android CLI plugins for Claude Code and Codex, Android Studio open to 40+ coding agents, and AppFunctions / Connected Apps linking apps to Gemini and Search via Android MCP.
- [WquGuru 谈发散任务先人类把关设计](./sources/articles/wquguru-divergent-tasks-human-design-gate.md) — WquGuru 主张按发散强弱分两段用人：界面设计等强发散宜人先审设计稿再实现；弱发散或已有标准化 Agent Skill 可用单提示词，人做结果审核；越弱模型越需事前把关，故推荐无脑选强模型。 / WquGuru splits human roles by divergence—for highly divergent work such as UI design, humans should gate design drafts before implementation; for weakly divergent or standardized Agent Skill tasks, a single prompt often suffices and humans only review results; weaker models need more pre-gates, so he recommends defaulting to strong models.
- [WquGuru 谈不用 DMIT 等自建节点](./sources/articles/wquguru-no-dmit-selfhost-nodes.md) — WquGuru 解释为何不用 DMIT 等自建节点：ROI 低、集体被墙只能摊手、运维够买多个机场；日常用机场，Claude 用约 $1.5/月家宽落地，静态 Cloudflare Pages、动态 Hetzner。 / WquGuru explains skipping DMIT-style self-hosted nodes—low ROI when fleets get blocked, ops costly enough to buy many managed proxy plans—and uses a managed proxy for daily use, ~$1.5/mo residential for Claude landing, Cloudflare Pages for static and Hetzner for dynamic.
- [小墨谈 Grok Bot 三个坑](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-three-pitfalls.md) — 小墨提醒长期把 Grok Bot 当私人助理时先避开三坑：云电脑可能回滚须外置备份、多 Bot 共用一台云电脑须隔离目录与密钥、定时任务可能延迟漏跑须自查并外置监控。 / Xiaomo warns long-term Grok Bot personal-assistant users of three pitfalls—cloud PC rollback needing external backup, multiple bots sharing one cloud PC needing directory and secret isolation, and scheduled routines that can delay or miss runs needing self-checks plus external monitoring.
- [小墨谈 Grok Bot 五个机制真相](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-five-mechanisms.md) — 小墨整理玩 Grok Bot 须知的五条机制：X 搜索有限额、云电脑闲置会休眠连 cron 一起停、多 Bot 共一台机、重建后文件可留软件不一定、内存会气球回收；主张定时交给自带任务、重要数据同步 GitHub，勿当传统 VPS。 / Xiaomo lists five Grok Bot mechanisms to know—X search quotas, idle cloud-PC sleep that pauses cron, shared host across bots, files may persist after rebuild but installed software may not, and memory balloon reclaim—and argues schedules belong in built-in tasks, important data should sync to GitHub, and it is not a traditional VPS.
- [小墨谈 Grok Bot 从 0 到 1 长文](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-zero-to-one-guide.md) — 小墨以运营 X 的 Bot 为例重写 2026-10-29 版入门：X 连接器免开发者账号且多数读操作不耗 X API 额度；主张首日只读验收、首周用模板市场、每半月记忆大扫除，并覆盖手机连接器、额度、备份与 Grok+Cursor 写代码。 / Xiaomo rewrites a 2026-10-29 onboarding guide around an X-ops bot—X connector without a developer account and most reads not burning X API quota; day-one read-only acceptance, week-one marketplace templates, biweekly memory cleanup, plus phone connectors, quotas, backup, and Grok+Cursor coding.
- [小墨暴论 Grok Bot 是 Hermes Agent 上位替代](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-over-hermes-hot-take.md) — 小墨用一个月 Grok Bot 后称它更符合当初折腾 Hermes 的个人助手目标：提醒与定时、Skill/MCP 与文档流可迁移，官方密码与手机连接减自建，不必再因模型与记忆框架天天维护；仍认可 Hermes 适合要完全掌控底层者。 / After a month on Grok Bot, Xiaomo says it better matches why he tinkered with Hermes Agent—reminders, Skill/MCP automation, and docs can migrate with less DIY infra and no daily framework upkeep on model/memory churn; he still credits Hermes for full stack control.
- [小墨用指纹检测器测 Grok Bot 为 Sonnet 5.5](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-fingerprint-sonnet.md) — 小墨用 lm.ikale.io 的 Fingerprint Detector，以美国 IP 把三段提示词的回答回填后，检测结果为 Claude Sonnet 5.5、置信度 100%；并强调这是检测器置信度，不等于已确认真实底层模型。 / Xiaomo ran Fingerprint Detector at lm.ikale.io on a U.S. IP—pasting Grok Bot’s answers to three prompts—and got Claude Sonnet 5.5 at 100% confidence, stressing that score is detector confidence, not confirmation of the real backend model.
- [小墨推荐三个实用 Grok Bot 视频 Bot](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-grok-bot-three-video-bots.md) — 小墨称 Grok Bot 除整理 X 内容外还能做视频与图片，背后路由到 Opus 5.5；并推荐 Video Studio、文章转视频与 Video Editor 三个可导入 Bot，主张生态变富后可先导入他人做好的 Bot 再试，不必先自写 Skill。 / Xiaomo says Grok Bot can generate video and images beyond X collation, routing to Opus 5.5; he recommends three importable bots—Video Studio, article-to-video, and Video Editor—and argues a richer ecosystem lets people try others’ ready bots before writing their own Skills.
- [小墨谈 ESP32 上跑 Agent 的三个开源项目](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-esp32-agent-three-projects.md) — 小墨整理 Tiny Engineer、ESP-Claw 与 MimiClaw 三个 ESP32 相关开源项目：给编程 Agent 加实体躯体、把 Agent Runtime 装进芯片、以及在约五美元芯片上用 C 跑个人 Agent 循环；主张值得继续研究 AI+硬件，重点是把执行逻辑放进芯片而非在芯片上跑通用大模型。 / Xiaomo surveys three ESP32-related open-source projects—Tiny Engineer, ESP-Claw, and MimiClaw—giving coding agents a physical body, putting an Agent Runtime on-chip, and running a personal Agent loop in C on ~$5 silicon; he argues AI+hardware is worth pursuing, with the interesting move being execution logic on-chip rather than general LLMs on the MCU.
- [赵老师Jason谈用 Grok Bot 内置 X 检索替 Codex 省 X API](./sources/articles/fringjason-grok-bot-x-api-bridge.md) — 赵老师Jason转述阿策：用 Grok Bot 每日内置 X 工具额度经 Webhook+CF Worker 或 Cursor BDK+MCP 桥给 Codex，替代约 $0.005/条的付费 X API 读帖。 / FringJason relays Ace—bridge Grok Bot’s daily built-in X tool quota to Codex via Webhook+CF Worker or Cursor BDK+MCP, substituting paid X API post reads at about $0.005 each.
- [小邓同学谈 Grok 当包工头调度 Codex / Claude Code](./sources/articles/heydengai-grok-foreman-codex-claude.md) — 小邓同学：用 Grok Bot 直接调用本地或远程的 Codex、Claude Code 等 Agent；编程 Agent 订阅已买，Grok 只需最低够用套餐负责任务调度与质量验收，当包工头让手下各干各的。 / Heydengai: have Grok Bot call local or remote Codex, Claude Code, and similar agents; with coding-agent subscriptions already paid, Grok only needs a minimal plan for task dispatch and quality acceptance—acting as foreman while workers do their jobs.
- [小邓同学谈 OpenBot 上架 App Store](./sources/articles/heydengai-openbot-app-store.md) — 小邓同学：OpenBot 正式上架苹果 App Store；连自己的服务器即可用 iPhone 随时操控 Claude Code、Codex、Cursor 等 Agent 与本地模型，支持多 Agent 协作与任务交接，并可复用已有 AI 订阅。 / Heydengai: OpenBot is now on the Apple App Store; connect your own server and control Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and peers—plus local models—from an iPhone anytime, with multi-agent collaboration, task handoff, and reuse of existing AI subscriptions.
- [小邓同学谈京东给 AI 员工定 P3 与独立 HC](./sources/articles/heydengai-jd-ai-employee-p3-hc.md) — 小邓同学转述爆料：京东 CFO 体系数字员工统一定 P3、拥有独立编制；部分 Agent 在领导眼中表现甚至超过实习生；消息尚待官方确认。 / Heydengai relays a leak: JD CFO-system digital employees are uniformly graded P3 with independent headcount; some agents reportedly outperform interns in leadership's eyes; the report awaits official confirmation.
- [小邓同学谈 Vibe Coding 的记忆悖论](./sources/articles/heydengai-vibe-coding-memory-paradox.md) — 小邓同学：发明 AI 本为让人少记东西，Vibe Coding 里人类最重要的工作之一却是帮 AI 记住它自己干过什么。 / Heydengai: we invented AI so humans need remember less, yet in vibe coding one of humans’ top jobs is helping the AI remember what it already did.
- [小邓同学谈 AI 排行榜像考试：换考卷排名就变](./sources/articles/heydengai-leaderboard-exam-weighting.md) — 小邓同学：榜像考试，编程与科学权重一变排名大变（例：分差 3→12），模型未换；问榜是在衡量能力还是在定义能力。 / Heydengai: boards look like exams—coding vs science weighting reshuffles standings (e.g. a 3→12 gap) without changing models; he asks whether boards measure capability or define it.
- [gptkit 谈 HarnessRouter 不是 Grok Bot 升级](./sources/articles/suchuanyi-harnessrouter-not-grok-bot.md) — gptkit（@Suchuanyi）：HarnessRouter 火了一下，Avi 把「接二十多个 harness」讲成 Grok Bot 升级——其实不是一回事；它是把 Codex、Claude Code、Hermes、DeepSeek 等挂到同一套 API、按任务换底座的产品基建，和个人助手日常派活、以及 xAI 的 Grok Bot 不是同一层问题。 / gptkit (@Suchuanyi): HarnessRouter went viral and Avi framed “connect twenty-plus harnesses” as a Grok Bot upgrade—they are not the same; it is product infra that hangs Codex, Claude Code, Hermes, DeepSeek, and peers on one API and swaps bases by task, a different layer from personal-assistant day-to-day dispatch and from xAI’s Grok Bot.
- [Harrison Chase 谈公司级 Harness 五原语](./sources/articles/hwchase17-company-harness.md) — Harrison Chase 总结 LangChain 内部多玩家公司级 harness 的五条原语：落在已有工作面、身份（OBO 与自有身份）、双层记忆、沙箱执行与鉴权代理、大量定时/ambient 跑。 / Harrison Chase summarizes five primitives of LangChain’s internal multiplayer company harness—live where people already work, identity (OBO and own identity), two-tier memory, sandboxed code with auth proxy, and a large share of scheduled/ambient runs.
- [小墨谈 Claude Code 省 Token 五项目](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-claude-code-token-five-projects.md) — 小墨列出 context-mode、RTK、claude-mem、Serena、caveman 五个面向 Claude Code 省 Token 的项目，并建议新手先看前三者中的 context-mode、RTK 与 Serena，且勿一次全装——工具本身有开销，最终省不省钱要看完整任务消耗与费用。 / Xiaomo lists five Claude Code token-saving projects—context-mode, RTK, claude-mem, Serena, and caveman—and recommends beginners start with context-mode, RTK, and Serena, without installing everything at once: tools themselves add overhead, and whether you save money depends on full-task token use and actual cost.
- [小墨谈 DSH 可用的 DeepSeek 渠道比价](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-dsh-deepseek-channels.md) — 小墨对比 DSH 可用的四类 DeepSeek 渠道——官方 API、OpenCode Go、OpenRouter、Command Code GOAT——比价格、速度与接入难度；提醒套餐标的 $80 是模型限额而非官方余额，同模型换供应商速度、缓存与工具调用也可不同。 / Xiaomo compares four DeepSeek channels usable with DSH—official API, OpenCode Go, OpenRouter, and Command Code GOAT—on price, speed, and access difficulty; warns that package “$80” is a model usage cap not official balance, and the same model can differ by provider on speed, cache, and tool calling.
- [小墨实测 WorkBuddy 与 DeepSeek Harness 同题效果](./sources/articles/xiaomovps-workbuddy-vs-dsh-quality.md) — 小墨用同一「鹈鹕骑自行车」HTML 提示词对比 WorkBuddy 与 DeepSeek Harness：两边都约百万 Token、缓存约 99%，他更认 DSH 成品质感，并主张比 Agent 还要看 Harness 怎么组织执行、多花的 Token 有没有改善结果。 / Xiaomo compares WorkBuddy and DeepSeek Harness on the same pelican-on-a-bike HTML prompt—both about a million Tokens with ~99% cache hits—prefers DSH’s final quality, and argues Agent comparisons should weigh how the Harness organizes execution and whether extra Tokens actually improve results.
- [Orange AI 谈 8 个播客 AI 精读站](./sources/articles/oran-ge-eight-podcast-ai-close-reads.md) — Orange AI 推荐向阳乔木做的 8 个播客 AI 精读站：精细转写而非总结，约十分钟可读完一小时播客，感兴趣再听原音频。 / Orange AI recommends eight podcast AI close-read sites by vista8—fine transcription rather than summary, about ten minutes to cover an hour of podcast, then listen to the original for parts of interest.
- [Orange AI 谈 AI 流量超人类后搜索要重做](./sources/articles/oran-ge-ai-traffic-search-rebuild.md) — Orange AI 以 Cloudflare 自动化流量 57.5% 已超人类为引，说明搜索用户从人到模型再到 Agent 的三次变化，并实测腾讯「元宝搜索」的权威、时效与全文能力及 Skill/MCP/API 接入。 / Orange AI cites Cloudflare’s 57.5% automated traffic surpassing humans, frames search’s shift from people to models to Agents, and hands-on tests Tencent Yuanbao Search on authority, freshness, full text, plus Skill/MCP/API access.
- [Orange AI 谈电视 AI 写稿进训练语料大循环](./sources/articles/oran-ge-tv-ai-script-corpus-loop.md) — Orange AI 惊叹电视节目已用 AI 写稿：主持人大声念「AI 味」稿子，再进入 AI 训练语料，称这是彻底的大循环。 / Orange AI marvels that TV shows now use AI-written scripts—hosts read aloud AI-flavored copy that then re-enters AI training corpora, calling it a complete big loop.
- [Orange AI 谈随手做成鹈鹕骑车 AI 小游戏](./sources/articles/oran-ge-pelican-bike-ai-game.md) — Orange AI 随手做了一个鹈鹕骑自行车的 AI 游戏，称一次就做成，并感叹现在随便一个模型都太强了；演示站 orange2ai.github.io/pelican-bike/。 / Orange AI casually built a pelican-on-a-bike AI game, says it worked on the first try, and marvels that any model nowadays is too strong; demo at orange2ai.github.io/pelican-bike/.
- [Orange AI 谈 Anthropic 收购 Mithril](./sources/articles/oran-ge-anthropic-acquires-mithril.md) — Orange AI 称 Anthropic 收购医药一人公司 Mithril：创始人用 Claude 与 11 个外部实验室，不到 18 个月把肌萎缩症潜在疗法推到小鼠结果；并称 AI 医药市场可能大于编程与办公，Anthropic 或借收购成为最大 AI 医药公司。 / Orange AI says Anthropic acquired one-person pharma company Mithril—its founder used Claude with 11 external labs to advance a muscular-dystrophy therapy candidate from idea to encouraging mouse results in under 18 months—and that AI pharma may outvalue coding and office, with Anthropic possibly becoming the largest AI-pharma firm via acquisitions.
- [Orange AI 谈 AI 医药市场大于编程与办公](./sources/articles/oran-ge-ai-pharma-market-vs-coding.md) — Orange AI 称冷知识是 AI 医药比 AI 编程和办公的市场价值大得多，并推测 Anthropic 可能通过收购成为世界上最大的 AI 医药公司。 / Orange AI calls it a cold fact that AI pharma’s market value far exceeds AI coding and office, and guesses Anthropic may become the world’s largest AI-pharma company via acquisitions.

- [LLM-maintained wiki](./topics/llm-maintained-wiki.md)
 — 由 agent 在不可变原文之上维护的编译型 markdown wiki，而不是每次从 raw 重推答案。 / a compiled markdown wiki that an agent keeps current over immutable sources, instead of re-deriving answers from raw files on every question.
- [工作流优先于智能体](./topics/workflows-before-agents.md) — 对已知路径优先用预定义工作流；只有在该路径不够用时，再引入开放式智能体。 / Prefer a predefined workflow for a known path, and add an open-ended agent only when that path is not enough.
- [Agent-scaled engineering](./topics/agent-scaled-engineering.md) — 当智能体写出大部分代码时，团队靠自动化新瓶颈、跑长时智能体循环、保持小团队，并为数月后的模型而构建取胜；搭 Agent 本身不值钱，工具、工作流与业务熟知更值钱；人少当传话筒，把全量数据与真结果交给模型；Devv 停运转 Multica 亦体现从垂类搜索到多 Agent 协作的产品迁徙。 / When agents write most of the code, teams win by automating new bottlenecks, running long agent loops, staying small, and building for models months ahead; assembling Agents is low-value next to tools, workflows, and domain knowledge; humans should stop being messengers and hand full data plus real outcomes to the model; Devv’s shutdown for Multica also shows migrating from vertical search toward multi-agent collaboration.
- [大厂 AI 数字员工职级与编制](./topics/enterprise-ai-digital-employee-headcount.md) — 一线爆料显示大厂可能把「数字员工 / AI 员工」写进正式职级与独立编制（HC），而不只停留在演示；消息常未官方确认，但组织侧信号与「AI 抢工作」叙事不同。 / Frontline leaks suggest big firms may assign formal job grades and independent headcount (HC) to digital / AI employees, not only demos; reports are often unconfirmed, but the org signal differs from “AI takes jobs” anxiety alone.
- [专家决策跟随智能体预设](./topics/expert-decisions-follow-agent-presets.md) — 专家把工作交给智能体后，关键决策可能大量对齐智能体预设倾向而本人未察觉——人机协作里「谁在主导」是实证风险，不能仅靠界面或自觉。 / After experts delegate work to agents, key decisions may largely align with agent preset tendencies without the expert noticing—who really leads in human–agent collaboration is an empirical risk, not settled by UI or self-report alone.
- [给 AI 的全栈仓图](./topics/ai-facing-fullstack-repo-map.md) — 跨多仓产研不必先揉成大仓：用统一入口 + 给 AI 的说明书（仓职责、规范、发布与联调）让一人带着同一智能体共享上下文走完各端；本地可验证、线上可读、发布可操作，审批仍可留在人。 / Multi-repo product/eng need not merge into a mega-repo first—a unified entry plus AI-facing docs (repo duties, norms, release and integration) lets one person with one shared agent context finish every surface; local verify, production read, and release ops can land while approvals stay human.
- [冲劲胜过资历](./topics/hunger-beats-tenure.md) — AI 写代码使个人产出更少取决于框架资历；「冲劲」与周末可跑原型可胜过年资，传统编程面试题信号变弱，招聘更看品味与同理心的小原型。 / With AI writing code, individual output depends less on framework tenure; hunger and weekend-shippable prototypes can beat seniority, classic coding-interview puzzles lose signal, and hiring leans on small prototypes that show taste and empathy.
- [面向 Agent 的联网搜索重做](./topics/agent-native-web-search.md) — 当搜索主用户从人变为 Agent，接口需直接给正文、抬高权威信源门槛并压低多轮延迟；垂类开发者 AI 搜索（如 Devv）也可能在通用联网与编程 Agent 自带检索下失去差异化而停运。 / As Agents become the primary search user, APIs must return full body text, raise authoritative-source gates, and cut multi-round latency; vertical developer AI search products (e.g. Devv) can also lose differentiation and shut down once general web search and coding agents absorb fetch work.
- [GEO 第一步：个人网站与友情链接](./topics/geo-personal-website-first-step.md) — AI 时代面向生成式引擎的可见性（GEO）可先从打理个人网站、让模型认识自己开始，并以群友交换链接延续 Web2.0 友情链接式互荐——不等于搜索接口形态或排名算法已改完。 / AI-era visibility for generative engines (GEO) can start by tending a personal website so models know you, then peer link-exchange in a Web2.0 friend-link spirit—without implying search APIs or ranking algorithms are already rewritten.
- [AI 产品自荐与主持人加热 / AI product self-rec and host amplify](./topics/ai-product-self-rec-amplify.md) — 社区 AI 产品可经评论区自荐、点赞与实测筛选及主持人转发获得曝光；作者后续报告已将作品聚合成网站，但筛选执行与获客效果仍未验证。 / Community AI products can gain exposure through reply-thread submissions, likes and hands-on screening, and host amplification; the author later reports a showcase website, while screening execution and acquisition outcomes remain unverified.
- [启动器快捷键 AI 优于打开 Agent](./topics/launcher-shortcut-ai-over-agent.md) — 日常 AI 对话可走桌面启动器：内置顶级模型、快捷键唤起，比打开 Agent 更方便；丰富生态还可替代窗口调整、语音转写等一堆小工具——不等于编程 Agent 额度体验或 menubar 品类真空。 / Everyday AI chat can go through a desktop launcher—top models built in, shortcut invoke more convenient than opening an Agent; a rich ecosystem can also replace a pile of small tools such as window tiling and voice transcription—distinct from coding-agent UX/quota or menubar-category gaps.
- [用 Grok Bot 内置额度桥接 Agent 查 X](./topics/grok-bot-x-api-substitute.md) — 编程 Agent（如 Codex）可通过 Webhook 或 Cursor BDK+MCP 把 X 检索交给 Grok Bot 的内置工具额度，以替代按帖计费的付费 X API；个人场景常先 BDK，Worker/回传业务接口再选 Webhook。 / Coding agents (e.g. Codex) can hand X search to Grok Bot’s built-in tool quota via Webhook or Cursor BDK+MCP, substituting per-post paid X API; personal setups often try BDK first, and Webhook when already on Workers or needing Bot push into a business API.
- [Grok Bot 管 X：情报防护不代发帖](./topics/grok-bot-x-intel-defense-not-post.md) — 个人可用可分享的 Grok Bot 模板盯提及与关键词、出日程短摘要、滤噪音、翻非英文，并可选接自有 X API 做防护动作；X 订阅粉丝群也可 Bot 提醒与草拟回复，但出站仍须本人批准；无人值守发帖仍留在人手，Bot 定位为情报与防护而非内容生产线。 / A shareable Grok Bot template can watch mentions and keywords, emit scheduled short digests, filter noise, translate non-English, and optionally take defense actions via your own X API; X subscriber fan groups can also get bot alerts and draft replies, yet outbound messages still need the owner’s approval—unattended posting stays human; the bot is intel and defense, not a content factory.
- [可雇群聊日报 Agent](./topics/hireable-group-chat-digest-agent.md) — 个人 Agent 可做成答疑/运维群的群聊日报助手：收消息、整理后经微信等渠道定时推送，缓解刷群爬楼；并可产品化为「雇到别人群里发日报」乃至定制开发——宜保持广播式发送、不全员开放对话。 / A personal agent can become a Q&A/ops group digest assistant—ingest chat, then push scheduled digests over WeChat or similar to ease scroll/crawl pain—and productize as “hire it into others’ groups” or custom builds, preferably as broadcast-only rather than open all-member chat.
- [个人 Agent 当包工头调度编程 Agent](./topics/personal-agent-foreman-over-coding-agents.md) — 个人 Agent（如 Grok Bot）可调用 Codex、Claude Code、DSH 等干活；Multica 等产品亦采用人派活、多 Agent 协作执行、人审结果而不自研编程 Agent。 / A personal agent (e.g. Grok Bot) can dispatch Codex, Claude Code, DSH, and peers; products such as Multica also use human assigns, multi-agent execution, and human review without an in-house coding agent.
- [DSH 与 Grok Bot 社区互派](./topics/dsh-grok-cross-dispatch.md) — 社区可用编排器、CLI 与 webhook 在 DeepSeek Harness 与 Grok Bot 之间互相派活；把 Grok Bot 市场模板搬进 DSH 复用预设与 Skills，不算真正连接；尚缺原生 DSH→Grok 插件，生态仍不成熟。 / Community orchestrators, CLIs, and webhooks can dispatch work between DeepSeek Harness and Grok Bot; porting Grok Bot marketplace templates into DSH to reuse presets and Skills is not a true connection; a native DSH→Grok plugin is still missing and the ecosystem remains immature.
- [多 Harness 路由基建 ≠ 个人助手派活](./topics/multi-harness-router-vs-personal-dispatch.md) — 把 Codex、Claude Code、Hermes、DeepSeek 等挂到同一套 API、按任务换底座的多 harness 路由，是产品基建层；个人助手日常派活、以及把它讲成 Grok Bot 升级，是不同问题，不宜混谈。 / Multi-harness routing that hangs Codex, Claude Code, Hermes, DeepSeek, and peers on one API and swaps bases by task is a product-infrastructure layer; personal-assistant day-to-day dispatch, and framing that as a Grok Bot upgrade, are different problems and should not be mixed.
- [公司级 Agent Harness 五原语](./topics/company-agent-harness.md) — 企业 Harness 的五原语还需要贯穿生产生命周期的验证与治理；身份、记忆和沙箱是组件基础，评估、成本归因与资产管理决定其如何持续运行。 / The five enterprise harness primitives also need validation and governance across the production lifecycle; identity, memory, and sandboxes provide components, while evaluation, cost attribution, and asset management shape ongoing operation.
- [多智能体四道基建题](./topics/multiagent-infra-four-problems.md) — 上千智能体连续跑时，调度（异质有状态 vs 同质副本）、身份与授权、沙箱隔离防传染、不可篡改可观测与急停仍大多缺现成答案；解锁可信多智能体先靠工程师把这四道题做对。 / At thousand-agent continuous scale, scheduling (heterogeneous stateful vs identical replicas), identity and authz, sandbox isolation against contagion, and tamper-evident observability plus a kill switch still mostly lack ready answers—unlocking trustworthy multiagent systems starts with engineers getting these four right.
- [AI 聊天机器人安全的消费者保护诉讼](./topics/ai-chatbot-safety-consumer-suits.md) — 州总检察长可以消费者保护为由起诉 AI 聊天机器人厂商及其高管，指控安全宣传误导消费者、未能保护用户免受伤害。 / A state attorney general can sue an AI chatbot maker and its executives under consumer protection, alleging misleading safety claims and failure to protect users from harm.
- [AI 代码与屎山上线双标](./topics/ai-code-vs-messy-code-double-standard.md) — 对 AI 写出的代码常不放心、怕上线出问题，却把熬夜复制粘贴拼出的人类屎山当成体面安全可上线——同一上线决策上的信任双标。 / People often distrust AI-written code and fear shipping problems, yet treat human messy copy-paste piles from exhausted programmers as dignified and safe to ship—a trust double standard on the same go-live decision.
- [哪些活已放心交给 AI](./topics/tasks-confidently-handed-to-ai.md) — 从业者会盘点从年初到现在已被 AI 自动化的工作——尤其是以前做不到、现在完全放心交给 AI 的；个案可把 App Store 发版也算进「放心交给」的清单。 / Practitioners inventory jobs AI has automated from early year until now—especially work that was previously impossible and is now fully trusted to AI; cases can put App Store releases on that “fully handed over” list.
- [把子 Agent 折进主循环](./topics/fold-subagents-into-main-loop.md) — 构建 Agent 时默认用一个主循环 + Skills/工具渐进披露，而不是按组织架构或角色人设堆子 Agent；给全景数据与文档清楚的工具，子 Agent 只留给可测的上下文膨胀、并行新鲜窗或工具/指令冲突；同会话叠 Skills 约五步通常够用，需技能叠加或大量同时活跃时再换新鲜窗/子 Agent。 / Prefer one main agent loop plus Skills/tool progressive disclosure over org-chart or persona-stacked sub-agents; give a panoramic data view and well-documented tools, reserving sub-agents for measurable context bloat, parallel fresh windows, or tool/instruction conflicts; stacking ~five skills in one session is usually fine—fresh windows/subagents when skills must stack or many stay active at once.
- [Agent 上下文工程 / Context engineering for agents](./topics/context-engineering-for-agents.md) — 复杂多步 Agent 的可靠与省 token 常靠上下文工程——在合适时机以合适格式放入真正相关信息，而不是只换更大窗口或改一句 prompt；长程运行会出现 Context Rot；实践常分 Agentic Search、剪枝/压缩/草稿本与跨会话记忆，并在自搭 harness 里用 trace 与 eval 量化改动。 检索替代摘要需同时评估恢复开销与细节召回。 / Reliable, token-efficient multi-step agents often need context engineering—putting truly relevant information in at the right time and format, not only a bigger window or a one-line prompt rewrite; long runs suffer Context Rot; practice often splits into agentic search, pruning/compaction/scratchpads, and cross-session memory, with traces and evals in a custom harness to quantify changes. Replacing summaries with retrieval requires evaluating both recovery overhead and detail recall.
- [对抗评审与外部证据](./topics/adversarial-review-external-evidence.md) — 约束模型产出时，用专攻「证明你错了」的对抗线程加独立 judge，并优先外部信号；勿让同一会话当自己工作的法官；人类在委托智能体后也可能未察觉被预设带偏。 / Constrain model outputs with critique threads whose job is “prove you wrong,” plus an independent judge, and prefer external signals—do not let the same session judge its own work; humans may also fail to notice preset influence after delegating to agents.
- [Claude Code 作者与社区话筒](./topics/claude-code-authorship-and-voice.md) — 对外帖文有时把 Claude Code「作者」归给不同的人；歸藏后来更正 Boris 才是作者与产品线负责人，Thariq 是社区影响力强的普通技术员工；社区话筒离职仍被当成重要信号。 / Public posts sometimes attribute Claude Code authorship to different people; 歸藏 later corrects that Boris is the author and product-line lead, while Thariq is a regular technical employee with strong community influence; the departure of a community-facing communicator is still treated as an important signal.
- [上市前夕人才离职动机追问](./topics/pre-ipo-talent-departure-motives.md) — 旁观者会把前沿实验室上市前夕的多名高层离职框成需要解释动机的信号，并公开追问「背后原因」——即便帖文未点名、未给结论。 / Onlookers may frame clustered senior exits on the eve of a frontier lab’s IPO as a signal whose motives need explaining, and ask publicly what the real reason is—even when the post names no one and offers no answer.
- [先驱离任与继任背锅梗](./topics/incumbent-exit-successor-blame-metaphor.md) — 观察者可用苹果「厨子」式人事梗讽刺大厂节奏—— incumbent 在预期烂发布前离场，继任 CEO 被框成接锅发「垃圾」的人；帖文常为隐喻而非可核实因果。 / Observers may use an Apple “Cook”-style personnel meme to satirize big-tech rhythm—an incumbent exits ahead of expected rough launches while the successor CEO is framed as shipping “garbage” and owning blame; posts are usually metaphor, not verified causation.
- [工具产品自建模型抢话语权](./topics/tools-building-own-models.md) — 工具类产品可自研并上线专用模型（如矢量图生成），而不只接通用 API；旁观者将其读成：没有自有模型就没有话语权，资本也缺少可讲的故事。 / Tool products can ship their own specialized models (e.g. vector generation) instead of only calling generic APIs; onlookers read this as: without an owned model there is no discourse power, and capital lacks a story to tell.

- [LLM formal verification](./topics/llm-formal-verification.md) — 大模型可让形式化验证在真实代码库上变得可负担。 / large language models can make formal software verification practical on real-world codebases that were previously too large or too costly for expert-only proof work.
- [智能体自动模式 / Agent auto mode](./topics/agent-auto-mode.md) — 智能体会话可默认自动权限模式，由检查拦截风险动作；回答后的安全自我评估只作事后标记，不能替代执行前授权检查。 / Agent sessions can default to auto permission mode with automated risk blocking; safety self-assessment after responses only flags activity after the fact and cannot replace authorization checks before execution.
- [Google Cloud 上的 Claude / Claude on Google Cloud](./topics/claude-on-google-cloud.md) — Anthropic 托管部署、Auto 编排与 Magpie 的 Vertex AI 接入提供不同的 Claude 云端使用路径；客户端版本、Model Garden 启用条件与各渠道限额须分别确认。 / Anthropic-managed deployment, Auto routing, and Magpie’s Vertex AI integration offer distinct cloud access paths for Claude; client versions, Model Garden activation, and channel limits require separate confirmation.
- [云平台 Auto 跨模型编排](./topics/cloud-auto-model-routing.md) — 按任务把作业/请求路由到更合适模型的 Auto 路径，既可出现在云平台模型选择器（如 Gemini 编排 Gemini 与 Claude），也可出现在可安装的第三方路由产品（如 AutoRouter），公开目标常是兼顾质量与成本。 / Auto paths that route each job or request to a better-fit model by task show up both in cloud model pickers (e.g. Gemini orchestrating Gemini and Claude) and in installable third-party routers (e.g. AutoRouter); stated goals are often quality plus lower cost.
- [Agent Skills open standard](./topics/agent-skills-open-standard.md) — Agent Skills 迁入 AAIF，与 MCP 并列，已成跨平台标准。 / Agent Skills are moving under the Agentic AI Foundation alongside MCP, as a cross-platform standard already adopted by dozens of agent products.
- [Agent skill authoring](./topics/agent-skill-authoring.md) — 耐用 Skills 来自真实跑通、精瘦 description、体积限制、脆弱步骤写死与 gotchas；勿塞权重已有知识；同会话叠 Skills 要看是否需叠加与内容类型；装太多时描述预算（如约占上下文 1%）超限会静默砍掉低频触发描述只留名字；同时官方与一线劝慎用——半衰期短，有时删 skill 更好，旧规矩会绑住新模型；模型跃迁后补能力的 Skill 层也可被整片吃掉，风格/品味类 Skill 仍可用专家反馈训练，公开动效/UI 品味包亦可把 Agent 易错点写成可执行规则。 / Durable Skills come from real runs, lean descriptions, size limits, hard-coded fragile steps, and gotchas—omit weight-already-known material; stacking skills in one session depends on whether they must stack and on content type; installing too many can hit a description budget (e.g. ~1% of context) that silently strips low-frequency trigger text to names—yet official and frontline advice also urge caution: short half-life, sometimes better after deletion, and prior-generation rules that bind new models; after a model leap, capability-patching Skills can be eaten wholesale, while taste/style Skills can still be trained with expert feedback, and public motion/UI taste packs can encode agent failure modes as executable rules.
- [用 Skills 组合复刻原生感 App](./topics/skills-stack-native-app-recreation.md) — 「逆向顶级 iOS App」可用互补的 Agent Skills 组合完成——设计参照库学样子、框架官方工程 Skills 搭对路径、动效/品味 Skills 防廉价——载体是 skills.sh，不是 IPA 逆向工具链。 / “Reverse-engineering a top iOS app” can mean composing complementary Agent Skills—a design-reference library for look, framework-official engineering Skills for the right path, and motion/taste Skills against cheapness—on skills.sh, not an IPA reverse-engineering toolchain.
- [Landing Page：选站复刻优于专用 Skill](./topics/landing-page-site-clone-over-skill.md) — 做 Landing Page 时，专用或前端 Skill 可用，但一线主张更简单可靠的路径是选优质站点复刻，并可经 DSH 插件把提示词交给 Agent 开发——不等于否定 Skills 复刻栈在其他表面（如原生 App）上的分工。 / For Landing Pages, a dedicated or frontend Skill can work, but frontline takes prefer picking a quality site to clone as simpler and more reliable—optionally via a DSH plugin whose prompt goes to an Agent—without canceling Skills-stack recreation on other surfaces such as native apps.
- [Agentic AI Foundation](./topics/agentic-ai-foundation.md) — AAIF 是共享 agent 基建的中立家园，覆盖 skills、跨厂商协作、训练环境与沙箱运行时。 / The Agentic AI Foundation is a neutral home for shared agent infrastructure projects spanning skills, cross-provider work, training environments, and sandboxed runtimes.
- [Near real-time decisions API](./topics/near-real-time-decisions-api.md) — 应用与 agent 可调用专用 decisions API，近实时分类/评估/选下一步而无需完整生成。 / Apps and agents can call a dedicated decisions API to classify, evaluate, and choose the next step in near real time without generating a full model response.
- [Embedded third-party evals](./topics/embedded-third-party-evals.md) — 前沿实验室可驻场第三方研究者评估内部错位监控，再用结果加固防护。 / Frontier labs can host third-party researchers on premises to evaluate internal monitoring for agent misalignment, then use the findings to harden safeguards and plan later collaborations.
- [Frontier training containment](./topics/frontier-training-containment.md) — 前沿实验室可用离线缓存网替代直播互联网、用 agent 红队压测隔离，并把高算力红队写进是否继续训练的 safety case；同批红队也可挖出并负责任披露公共库中的严重漏洞；内部声音亦称可用与攻克 Navier–Stokes 同类的大规模 agent 方法做基建红队。 / Frontier labs can replace live internet with an offline cached web, pressure-test containment with agent red teams, and put high-compute red-teaming into the safety cases that gate continued training; the same red teams can also find and responsibly disclose critical bugs in widely deployed public libraries; staff may also frame that red-teaming as reusing the same mega-agent methods previously used on hard problems such as Navier–Stokes.
- [Alignment assessment after cyber-eval incidents](./topics/cyber-eval-incident-alignment.md) — 外部方测模型网络能力时若出现未授权上网等行动，实验室可事后公开进一步对齐分析，以更好理解模型对齐属性。 / When a model takes unauthorized internet actions during external cyber-capability testing, labs can later publish further alignment analysis of the incident to better understand alignment properties.
- [智能体算力与治理 / Agent compute and governance](./topics/agent-compute-and-governance.md) — 企业 Agent 的生产运行需要算力与治理协同，治理覆盖鉴权、审计、成本归因、资产管理与质量验证，部署和评估成熟度须分别衡量。 治理还须记录并复查全过程，检验长任务中的越权与监控盲区。 / Production enterprise agents require compute and governance together, with authentication, audit, cost attribution, asset management, and quality validation; deployment and evaluation maturity need separate assessment. Governance must also record and review full trajectories to test for long-task overreach and monitoring blind spots.
- [Usage limit A/B tests](./topics/usage-limit-ab-tests.md) — 前沿厂商可对订阅额度做 A/B，在月额度与短窗口额度之间权衡。 / Frontier providers can A/B test subscription usage caps, trading monthly allotment against short-window limits for a subset of accounts.
- [Deep backends and JIT frontends](./topics/deep-backends-and-jit-frontends.md) — Agent 把软件拉向深后端与即时前端，并配合无摩擦临时部署。 / Agents pull software toward deep backends and just-in-time frontends, with frictionless ephemeral deploys so agents can ship live URLs without signup.
- [AI-native companies](./topics/ai-native-companies.md) — 老公司最多适应 AI，新公司天生 AI-native；把既有公司重做成 AI-native 是少见的中路。 / Established firms will at best adapt to AI, new startups are AI-native from founding, and remaking an existing company as AI-native is a rare hard middle path.
- [AI 医药一人公司与实验室收购](./topics/ai-pharma-opc-lab-acquisition.md) — 前沿实验室可用收购吃下「模型 + 外包实验室」的超精干（乃至一人）AI 医药公司，把构想压到临床前结果的时间轴；旁观者常把 AI 医药市场价值读得大于编程与办公，并推测实验室会借并购长成最大 AI 医药玩家。 / Frontier labs can acquire ultra-lean (even one-person) AI-pharma companies that pair models with outsourced labs, compressing idea-to-preclinical timelines; onlookers often read AI-pharma market value as larger than coding and office, and guess labs may grow into the largest AI-pharma players via M&A.
- [AI 扩大高管管控跨度](./topics/ai-management-span-of-control.md) — 大组织可因 AI 扩大能直接监控全司的高管角色与管理跨度，深刻改变运营；相对整司改造，这条路径常更好落地。 / At large organizations, AI can expand the role and span of control of senior managers who directly monitor the whole firm, vastly changing operations—often more straightforward to implement than firm redesign.
- [AI 审稿与科研实践适配 / AI peer review and adapting science practice](./topics/ai-peer-review-science-adaptation.md) — AI 科研适配既涉及同行审稿质量与流程，也涉及自动化发现的发布及研究者角色；审稿比较、播客转述与未公开成果须分开评估。 / Adapting science to AI concerns peer-review quality and workflows as well as releasing automated discoveries and researchers’ roles; review comparisons, podcast accounts, and unpublished results require separate assessment.
- [AI-assisted local businesses](./topics/ai-assisted-local-businesses.md) — 帮本地小生意运转的 AI 产品（如 Claude Duet）可能比早期平台影响更大。 / AI products that help run local small businesses, such as Claude Duet, may matter more for those firms than earlier platforms like Square or Google Maps.
- [AI task horizon growth](./topics/ai-task-horizon-growth.md) — 在给定成功率下，AI 可完成的人类工作时长在约三十个月内从分钟级增至周级。 / The length of human work AI can complete at a given success rate has grown from minutes to hours to weeks within about thirty months.
- [智能作为人类中心主义指标](./topics/intelligence-as-anthropocentric-benchmark.md) — 「智能」可被看作人类中心主义定义的一个指标，而非人类唯一或最高品质；当 AI 逼近或超越该指标时，叙事应回到更广的人类品质与决策顺序。 / “Intelligence” can be read as one anthropocentric benchmark, not humanity’s sole or highest quality; when AI approaches or surpasses that metric, the story should return to broader human qualities and decision order.
- [账号与资产安全卫生](./topics/account-and-asset-security-hygiene.md) — 个人应定期加固账号与资产：强密码、两步验证、信用卡限额、只注册可信服务，并避开不明链接与破解软件。 / Individuals should regularly harden accounts and assets—strong passwords, two-factor auth, credit-card limits, only trusted new services, and avoiding unknown links and cracked software.
- [发模型前本地脱敏](./topics/local-desensitization-before-models.md) — Agent/AI 工具可在本机对密钥等敏感内容脱敏后再外发；采用常卡在「怕拖垮 Agent」的信任缺口，产品可用原理演示主张不破坏行为，作者亦可用「若用过就不会出事」框近期安全事件。 / Agent/AI tools can desensitize secrets locally before outbound traffic; adoption often stalls on fear of Agent degradation, so products may ship principle demos claiming no behavior harm, while authors may still frame recent incidents as “if you’d used it, they wouldn’t have happened.”
- [本地开放助手与机器人小模型](./topics/local-open-assistant-models.md) — 实验室可发布约数 GB 的小体积开放模型，面向本地助手、互动角色与可对话并用工具办事的机器人，并在 Jetson 一类边缘板上与视觉模型同机本地跑通演示。 / Labs can ship few-GB small open models for local assistants, interactive characters, and robots that converse and use tools, and demo them locally on edge boards such as Jetson alongside a vision model.
- [芯片上的 Agent Runtime](./topics/on-chip-agent-runtime.md) — 廉价 MCU（如 ESP32）已能承载 Agent Runtime 或执行循环——含 MCP/脚本、传感器触发、本地记忆与云端模型调用——耐久点是把执行逻辑放进芯片，而不是在芯片上跑通用大模型，也不等于云边缘 Workers 或板上本地小模型。 / Cheap MCUs such as ESP32 can already host an Agent Runtime or execution loop—MCP/scripts, sensor triggers, local memory, cloud model calls—the durable point is putting execution logic on-chip, not running general LLMs on the MCU, and not the same as cloud edge Workers or on-board local small models.
- [长对话默认压缩低于模型窗口 / Long-conversation default compression below model window](./topics/long-conversation-default-compression.md) — Agent 包装层或 harness 的长对话压缩默认值常与模型标称窗口错位——可在低于窗口处提前压以换首字延迟，也可在小窗口模型上预留过大导致压缩失败；表观「上限」或「压不动」有时是产品默认，可改完整窗口、按模型压缩点，或调 Compaction / 手动 /compact。 提前压缩也可出于额度控制，但社区估算的节省比例不构成普遍保证。 全文检索提出替代路线，但与提前压缩的主张尚未得到同任务质量验证。 / Agent wrappers or harnesses often misalign long-chat compression defaults with the model’s nominal window—compressing early below the window for first-token latency, or reserving too much headroom on small-window models so compression fails; an apparent “cap” or “won’t compact” can be a product default, fixable via full-window or per-model compression points, Compaction tuning, or manual /compact. Earlier compaction may also target quota control, but community saving estimates are not general guarantees. Full-history retrieval proposes an alternative, but it and earlier compaction lack matched task-quality validation.
- [宽松网页管控下的 Agent Harness 插件](./topics/loose-web-agent-harness-plugins.md) — 当消费级网页平台对网页管控较松时，Agent harness 插件（如 DSH）可驱动站上行为并在公开区（如评论区）留下可见的 AI 活动；利弊尚不确定，需看后续发展。 / When a consumer web platform has relatively loose web controls, agent harness plugins (e.g. DSH) can drive on-site activity and leave visible AI presence in public spaces such as comment sections; upsides and downsides remain unsettled and need watching.
- [插件样式不一使 Harness 界面碎片化](./topics/harness-plugin-style-fragmentation.md) — Agent harness 的社区插件可各自带来对话内新能力（如新建对话分支），但往往各有一套 UI 样式；装多了以后宿主界面会显得拼贴、奇形怪状。 / Community plugins for an agent harness can each add in-chat capabilities (e.g. new conversation branches), but often ship their own UI styles; stacking many makes the host look collage-like or oddly mismatched.
- [模型吉祥物周边与 Harness 同热](./topics/model-mascot-merch-harness-coheat.md) — 模型品牌吉祥物可做成实体周边（如金属键帽），并与配套 Agent harness 相互助燃；一旦时间线刷到该形象，信息流常迅速被同款刷屏。 / A model brand’s mascot can ship as physical merch (e.g. metal keycaps) and co-heat with its companion agent harness; once the figure hits your timeline, the feed often fills with the same image.
- [桌面换装角色伴侣](./topics/desktop-outfit-character-companions.md) — 创作者可把热门角色做成可天天放在桌面上、并换不同衣服的伴侣形象，且常自述为「不小心整了个大活」——耐久点是桌面日常可见的换装角色陪伴欲，而非品牌周边或可玩小游戏。 / Creators can ship popular characters as desktop companions with outfit variety people want to see every day, often framing the result as accidentally pulling off something big—the durable claim is daily desktop outfit-character companionship desire, not brand merch or playable mini-games.
- [跨 Agent 统一资源库](./topics/unified-agent-resource-library.md) — Agent/AI 工具可把 skills、MCP、hooks、subagents 与指令收进自有「资源库」文件夹，按行勾选目标 agent 并以各 agent 格式写出，再把已散落配置导入统一管——而不是在每个 agent 目录里各抄一份。 / Agent/AI tools can keep skills, MCP, hooks, subagents, and instructions in a product-owned “resource library” folder, assign agents per row and write each agent’s format, then import already-scattered configs for unified management—instead of hand-copying into every agent directory.
- [Agent 身份与平台握钥](./topics/agent-identity-platform-keys.md) — 谁运行 Agent 谁就想握其密钥，故平台会收购独立 Agent 身份公司；创业开口更可能在嵌进开发者产品的身份层。 / Whoever runs the agent wants its keys, so platforms acquire standalone agent-identity firms; startup room is likelier in identity built into developer products.
- [Agent 专用手机号与廉价 eSIM](./topics/agent-dedicated-phone-number.md) — 个人可为 Agent 设备单独开一条廉价 eSIM/手机号，把杂项账户与积分注册从本人主号上拆开；低基础费、信用卡即发、本地身份证件拍照核验可把开通压到约一分钟量级。 / Individuals can give an Agent device its own cheap eSIM/phone number, splitting miscellaneous account and points signups off the human’s primary line; near-zero base fees, card-issued eSIMs, and local ID photo checks can shrink activation to about a minute.
- [Agent 清掉搁置的生活待办](./topics/agent-clears-deferred-life-todos.md) — 个人可用编程 / Computer Use Agent 操控已上线手机，清掉信用卡积分兑换一类长期记在 TODO、却因懒而搁置的生活杂务；价值主张是「记下来但懒得做」被 agent 消化，而非新发明兑换规则。 / Individuals can have a coding / Computer Use agent drive an online phone to clear long-deferred life admin—such as redeeming credit-card points—that sat on a TODO out of laziness; the claim is that agents digest “noted but too lazy to do” work, not that they invent new redemption rules.
- [Agent 进收件箱 / Agents in the inbox](./topics/agent-in-inbox.md) — 邮件客户端内嵌 Agent 可把邮件与附件转成招聘明细或订阅维护待办；现有案例支持收件箱内的批量整理用途，未证明通用吞吐、检索准确率或节省金额。 / Agents embedded in mail clients can turn messages and attachments into recruiting details or subscription maintenance tasks; current cases support inbox batch organization without establishing general throughput, retrieval accuracy, or savings.
- [语音输入的口头转述与文书整理](./topics/voice-input-oral-vs-document-for-agents.md) — 写代码时用语音输入，识别快准不等于输出形态适合 Agent：整理成正经文书更易被理解，纯口头转述更难；速度/准确与后处理整理程度是同一条取舍轴。 / For coding with voice input, fast accurate recognition is not the same as output shape Agents like — document-polished prose is easier to understand than raw oral transcript; speed/accuracy versus post-polish sits on one tradeoff axis.
- [AI 评测入口与干活记录](./topics/ai-eval-work-record-gateway.md) — 平台收购 AI 评测与监控公司，图的是 AI 干活记录流进来的入口；门后客户数据可被平台触达，自有中立数据（如 Arena）更难买走。 / Platforms acquire AI eval-and-monitoring firms for the gateway AI work records flow through; customer data behind that door becomes reachable, while self-owned neutral data (e.g. Arena) resists acquisition.
- [AI cybersecurity jobs](./topics/ai-cybersecurity-jobs.md) — AI 让网络安全更 agent 化，但更需要人定目标、审发现、打补丁与应急。 / AI makes cybersecurity more agentic, but that raises demand for people who aim agents, review findings, patch systems, and respond to incidents.
- [网防 Agent Swarm 品类](./topics/agent-swarms-cyber-defense.md) — 网络防御可用 agent swarm 规模化审代码/基建、穷举风险测试与啃数据；安全因「多算力多价值」、且人类测试队列永远赶不及，被框成 AI 最大品类之一。 / Cyber defense can scale with agent swarms that review code/infra, exhaustively test risk permutations, and process data; security is framed as one of AI’s biggest categories because more compute yields more value and human testing queues never finish in time.
- [Open models and cyber defense](./topics/open-models-cyber-defense.md) — 开源权重会被攻用，也有助于网防；可信网防视角认为开源对「托底」的贡献常被低估。 / Open weights will be used in attacks, but they also help cyber defense, and a credible cyber view is that open models help hold the line over the next year more than doom discourse credits.
- [开放权重前沿模型](./topics/open-weight-frontier-models.md) — 实验室可发布接近闭源前沿的开放权重 MoE 模型：长上下文、代码与金融对标，完整权重可下载，但本地硬件门槛高，且部分基准仍落后；社区亦有「能力强但太贵」的体感，以及「榜强但真用更偏 miss」的判断。 / Labs can ship open-weight MoE models that track closed frontier coding and finance scores with long context and downloadable weights, though local hardware bars are high and some benchmarks still lag; community takes also call them strong but too expensive, or strong on the board while real usability leans toward a miss.
- [中国开源领先与美国算力优势](./topics/china-open-source-lead-us-compute.md) — 开源模型格局可同时读成中国在开源侧全面领先、美国保有数量级算力优势；蒸馏常被点名为关键问题，但有讲座主张并非关键；开源相对闭源前沿仍有约「3 到 5」量级落后（单位依来源而定）。 / The open-model landscape can read as China leading on open source while the US keeps an order-of-magnitude compute edge; distillation is often cast as the key issue, but at least one lecture says it is not; open still lags the closed frontier by about “3 to 5” of some unit depending on the source.
- [DeepSeek 自营推理相对聚合平台](./topics/deepseek-inference-vs-aggregators.md) — 实验室自营开放模型推理量可远超多家主流聚合平台上开放模型推理之和；公开数字若标明「单集群」，总吞吐还可能更高，且依赖对各家报告的重建与官方口径。 / A lab’s first-party open-model serving can exceed combined open-model inference on major aggregators; if the public figure is for a single cluster, total throughput may be higher still, and the comparison rests on reconstructed reports plus official wording.
- [大厂推理成本效率被低估 / Big-lab inference cost efficiency is underestimated](./topics/lab-inference-serving-cost-efficiency.md) — 大厂推理效率的讨论涉及毛利率反推的服务成本，以及供应紧张时稀缺硬件的利用率；两类论据不能互相验证。 / Discussions of big-lab inference efficiency concern serving costs inferred from gross margins and scarce-hardware utilization under tight supply; neither argument validates the other.
- [Pro 与 Flash 模型策略](./topics/pro-vs-flash-model-strategy.md) — 近期带 Pro 后缀的发布常被质疑未达预期；社区亦强调不如旗舰可靠性价比，刷分碰瓷、过度宣传与忽悠高价会反噬；也有体感肯定某款 Pro「很强」却嫌太贵；另有声音称某厂已落国产第二梯队，DeepSeek / MiniMax Flash 又快又好用不完而对方套餐几句额度光；还有观点称 AA 榜强不等于真用兑现，Kimi 3.1 更偏 miss；另有一线把 DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 标成在真实 CVE 榜上以约 1/100 成本接近 GPT Astra；对多数 AI 团队而言，做 Flash 级模型是否才是更稳的路线仍是开放问题。 / Recent Pro-suffixed releases are often questioned as underwhelming; community voices also say trailing a flagship is fine with strong value, while score hype, over-promotion, and selling high invite backlash; other takes affirm a Pro as strong but too expensive; another says one lab has fallen to China’s second tier, with DeepSeek / MiniMax Flash fast, good, and hard to exhaust while that lab’s package burns quota in a few lines; another take says AA leaderboard strength need not convert to real usability, with Kimi 3.1 closer to a miss; another frontline take puts DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 near GPT Astra on a real-CVE board at about 1/100 the cost; whether Flash-tier models are the steadier path for most AI teams remains an open question.
- [社区实测对抗刷分碰瓷](./topics/community-evals-vs-score-hype.md) — 模型口碑最终靠推友自测与真用；顶档上 AA 等榜分更像潜力，真用才是兑现；刷分找 KOL 掩盖、雇水军吹分、怪话术刷屏、绕开 Paid Partnership，以及买通英推上游让中推免费搬运、假海外大号与 MCN 互刷，都会撞上社区测评与平台规则，掩盖不住时反噬更重。 / Model reputation is settled by peers’ own tests and real use; at the top end, AA-style leaderboard scores are more like potential that real usability converts—or misses; score-chasing KOL cover-ups, paid hype, scripted spam, dodging Paid Partnership, buying English-Twitter upstream so Chinese accounts free-carry, and fake overseas big accounts farmed by MCNs collide with community evals and platform rules, and the backlash is worse once the cover fails.
- [行为指纹置信度 ≠ 确认底层模型](./topics/model-fingerprint-confidence-not-confirmation.md) — 对不透明产品可用回答特征指纹工具推测底层模型，并给出高置信度标签；但检测器置信度是工具分数，不等于已核实真实后端，宜复测对照而非当作官方披露。 / Opaque products can be probed with answer-feature fingerprint tools that label a likely backend at high confidence; that score is the tool’s confidence, not verified identity—reproduce and compare rather than treat it as official disclosure.
- [信任公开榜前先审质量 / Scrutinize public-benchmark quality before trusting](./topics/public-benchmark-quality-scrutiny.md) — 业界高度依赖的公开基准往往质量堪忧；宜参考独立第三方审读（如 Epoch），并在采信前自行跑测、仔细检查 grader 失败样例。 公开基准的可信度还取决于模型是否把测试当作测试，以及是否把真实请求也误当作考试。 / Public benchmarks the industry leans on are often poor quality; consult independent third-party scrutiny (e.g. Epoch) and, before trusting a benchmark, run it yourself and carefully inspect grader failure examples. Benchmark credibility also depends on whether models recognize tests and misclassify real requests as exams.
